Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the symmetric object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 843. The moduli space has dimension at most 223 with 1 connected components.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to symmetric structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 843/223 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
